### 2026-06-26 UC-09 Share max access count closure

- 范围：`REQ-COLLAB-001`，外部分享链接的最大访问次数控制。
- 结论：分享链接现在支持 `maxAccessCount`；`0` 表示不限制，正整数会限制外部只读报告成功浏览次数。达到上限后，系统拒绝继续访问并写入 `share_access_limit_exceeded` 审计。
- 代码证据：`ShareLink.maxAccessCount`、Flyway `V040__share_link_max_access_count.sql`、`share_links.max_access_count`、`PermissionApplicationService.enforceShareAccessCountLimit()`、`AuditRepository.countByResourceOperationSince()`、报告详情页 owner 分享面板的“最大访问次数”输入、P1 smoke 的 `persistsShareMaxAccessCountAndEnforcesItInPostgres`。
- 验证证据：后端目标用例 `PermissionApplicationServiceTest#shareAccessCanBeLimitedByMaximumViewCount` RED 后 GREEN；PostgreSQL 集成测试 `JdbcReportGenerationTaskRepositoryPostgresIT#persistsShareMaxAccessCountAndEnforcesItInPostgres` 通过并迁移到 v040；`npm run test -- apiContracts` 25 条通过；`npm run typecheck` 通过；`npm run e2e -- report-generation.spec.ts --grep "创建、复制和撤销分享链接"` 通过。
- 剩余缺口：验证码/挑战机制、访问域名/邮箱范围、单次链接等更高阶分享策略仍需后续闭环。

### 2026-06-26 UC-09 Share visitor scope closure

### 2026-06-29 UC-08 Sequential multi-level approval closure

- 剩余缺口：当前闭合的是线性顺序多级审批；独立人工审批流设计器、驳回后的补件/重新提交业务闭环、子流程节点、多级 SLA 策略和更高阶画布约束仍需后续迭代。

### 2026-06-29 UC-08 Approval rejection supplement resubmission closure

- 范围：`REQ-RULE-001`，审批驳回后的补件要求、重新提交和新待审批记录生成。
- 结论：审批人驳回后，系统会保留原审批记录为 `rejected`，生成 `supplement_required` 补件记录；用户提交补件后，补件记录变为 `resubmitted`，并为同一运行节点创建新的 `pending` 审批记录，让业务流程可以从驳回态回到审批态。

- 剩余缺口：当前闭合的是后端状态机和接口契约；前端审批待办/审批详情仍需增加补件提交入口，独立人工审批流设计器和可复用审批模板仍需后续迭代。

### 2026-07-02 UC-08 Higress webhook automatic replay exhaustion closure

- Scope: `REQ-RULE-001`, `REQ-AUTH-001`, `UC-08`.
- Result: webhook compensation now covers the automatic replay exhaustion path. A browser production run can trigger a real webhook `503`, show `pending_retry`, wait for the Java replay worker to exhaust the chain, refresh the ledger, and see `compensation_exhausted` with `Source action #<original failed action id>`.
- Runtime path: browser -> Higress -> Java rule runtime -> webhook callback -> scheduled replay worker -> action compensation ledger.
- Traceability evidence: Java keeps the original `sourceActionExecutionId` across retry chains and stops duplicate terminal compensation rows once an idempotency chain has been exhausted.
- Code evidence: `RuleApplicationService.java`, `JdbcRuleRepository.java`, `InMemoryRuleRepository.java`, `RuleApplicationServiceTest.java`, `rule-runtime-real-backend.spec.ts`, `scripts/p3-local-smoke-lib.mjs`, and `docs/skill-chain/delivery_closure_295_uc08_higress_webhook_replay_exhaustion.md`.
- Verification evidence: RED first failed waiting for `compensation_exhausted`; backend RED then proved repeated exhaustion rows were possible. GREEN passed direct Java browser E2E `1/1`, Higress browser E2E `1/1`, backend rule service regression `88/88`, frontend API contract tests `30/30`, and Node P3/Higress smoke contract `6/6`.
- Remaining gaps: full P3 smoke bundle execution, broader endpoint-level Higress authorization matrix, and OIDC/TLS/WAF production hardening remain open.
